To our shareholders,                                                              致各股東：

In 2023, China’s economy rebounded and showed signs of improvement.               2023年中國經濟回升向好，轉型升級積極推

gross domestic product (“GDP”) growth rate for the year was 5.2%.                 幅下滑及落後產能退出較慢，水泥行業供需失
However, due to the sharp decline in real estate investment and slowdown          衡加劇，競爭激烈導致行業整體效益進一步下
in the phase-out of backward capacity, the imbalance between supply and           滑，行業利潤總額同比降幅超50%。
demand in the cement industry escalated. Fierce competition had led to a
further decline in the overall profitability of the industry, with total profit
of the industry dropping by more than 50% year-on-year.

cement industry. Following the joint publication of the “Implementation           聯合印發《建材行業碳達峰實施方案》，國家
Plan for Carbon Peak Achievement in the Building Materials Industry”              發改委《產業結構調整指導目錄（2023年本）》

restriction category and prohibition category of the industrial catalogue.        長工作方案》中提出研究推動水泥行業納入全

action plans or action plans for industry transformation and upgrading,           放評估監測程序和方法建立明細指導並統一標
further accelerating the clearance and phase-out of backward capacity.            準，因此增加的履約成本很可能由水泥企業自
At the same time, the “Building Materials Industry’s Work Plan to Ensure          身承擔，小企業迫於成本壓力或將加快出清，
Growth Stability” proposed to study and promote the inclusion of the              同時，政策的實施也將有益於推動企業加大綠
cement industry in the national carbon emissions trading market. It is            色低碳關鍵技術研發。
also setting out detailed guidelines and uniform standards for ultra-
low emissions assessment and monitoring procedures and methods. As
a result, the increased compliance costs are likely to be borne by the

technologies.

During the past year, Asia Cement (China) demonstrated its strengths              過去一年，亞泥（中國）秉持「誠、勤、樸、
and resilience in the face of adversity by upholding the business spirit of       慎、創新」的立業精神，於困境中展現出硬實

implementation of ultra-low emission modifications, and accelerated the           以此為核心拓展智慧化項目，覆蓋優化控制、
digitalization and intelligentization process. Together with Ding & Ding          品質管理、物流管理和設備管理等方面，持續

Management Discussion and Analysis
管理層討論及分析

I.   2023 BUSINESS REVIEW                                                 一、 二零二三年業務回顧
     (1)   Macro perspectives of China and the industry                     （一） 國家及行業宏觀概況

Corporate Governance Report
企業管治報告

The Board is committed to maintaining high standards of corporate               董事會致力維持高水平之企業管治常規，以保
governance practices to safeguard the interests of the Company’s                障本公司股東權益，並提升企業價值及問責
shareholders and to enhance corporate value and accountability. These           性。有關目標可透過高效率之董事會、問責清
can be achieved by an effective Board, segregation of duties with clear         晰且權責分明之職務、良好內部監控、適當風
accountability, sound internal control, appropriate risk assessment             險評估程序及對全體股東之透明度而達致。
procedures and transparency to all the shareholders.

Corporate governance is the process by which the Board instructs                企業管治是董事會指導集團管理層處理事務以
management of the Group to conduct its affairs with a view to ensuring          確保實現目標的過程。董事會致力於維護及發
that its objectives are met. The Board is committed to maintaining and          展穩健的企業管治實踐，以確保：
developing robust corporate governance practices that are intended to
ensure:

–      satisfactory and sustainable returns to shareholders;                    －    為股東提供滿意及可持續的回報；

–      that the interests of those who deal with the Company are                －    與公司有業務來往者的利益得到保障；
       safeguarded;

–      that overall business risk is understood and managed appropriately;      －    了解並適當地管理整體業務風險；
       and

–      the delivery of high-quality products and services to the satisfaction   －    提供顧客滿意的優質產品與服務；及
       of customers; and

–      that high standards of ethics are maintained.                            －    維持高商業道德標準。

The Company has complied with all the code provisions of the Corporate          本公司於截至2023年12月31日止之年度，一

Mechanism which sets out the processes and procedures to ensure                程及程序以確保董事會具有穩固的獨立性，讓
a strong independent element on the Board, which allows the Board              董事會能夠有效地作出獨立判斷，以更有效的
effectively exercises independent judgment to better safeguard                 保障股東利益。
Shareholders’ interests.

The objectives of the evaluation are to improve Board effectiveness,           評審的目標旨在改善董事會的效率、使能更好
maximise strengths, and identify the areas that need improvement or            體現其優勢及識別需改善或進一步發展的領
further development. The evaluation process also clarifies what actions        域。評審過程釐清本公司於維持及改善董事會
of the Company need to be taken to maintain and improve the Board              績效，例如，解決各董事的個人培訓和發展需
performance, for instance, addressing individual training and development      求。
needs of each Director.

GENDER DIVERSITY                                                            性別多元化
The Company values gender diversity across all levels of the Group. As of   本公司重視集團各職級別的性別多元化。截至

and internal control systems and for reviewing its effectiveness. The risk    及審閱其成效。風險管理及內部監控系統旨在

and related products. The activities of the principal subsidiaries are set     主要附屬公司之業務載於本年報第179至183

We have audited the consolidated financial statements of Asia Cement         我們已審計載於第64至187頁亞洲水泥（中國）
(China) Holdings Corporation (the “Company”) and its subsidiaries            控股公司（「貴公司」）及其附屬公司（以下統稱

which comprise the consolidated statement of financial position as at        月31日的綜合財務狀況表、截至該日止年度的
31 December 2023, and the consolidated statement of profit or loss           綜合損益及其他全面收益表、綜合權益變動表
and other comprehensive income, consolidated statement of changes in         及綜合現金流量表以及綜合財務報表附註（包
equity and consolidated statement of cash flows for the year then ended,     括主要會計政策概要）。

accounting policy information and other explanatory information.

December 2023, and of its consolidated financial performance and its         務報告準則（「國際財務報告準則」）真實而公允

 Key audit matters                                              關鍵審計事項

 Estimated provision of expected credit losses (“ECL”) for trade receivables
 交易應收款項的預期信貸損失評估

 With the impact of the property market recession and the       由於2023年房地產市場衰退及經濟復甦乏力影響，分
 sluggish recovery on the economy in 2023, the property         別作為本集團間接客戶和最終客戶的房地產開發商和建
 developers and the constructors who were the indirect          造商面臨龐大債務及現金流問題。截至2023年12月31
 and ultimate customers of the Group respectively faced         日，本集團應收款項扣除人民幣220,381,000元準備後
 massive debt and cash flow issues. As at 31 December           的淨額約為人民幣325,778,000元。於截至2023年12月
 2023, the trade receivables of the Group was approximately     31日止年度，本集團撥回人民幣66,843,000元的應收款
 RMB325,778,000, net of allowance amounting to                  項預期信貸虧損。
 RMB220,381,000. During the year ended 31 December
 2023, the Group reversed RMB66,843,000 of ECL of trade
 receivables.

 We identified impairment assessment of trade receivables       我們將交易應收款項的減值評估確認為一項關鍵審計事
 as a key audit matter due to the significance of trade         項，原因在於信用風險的增加及其結餘對綜合財務報表
 receivables to the Group’s consolidated financial position     整體極其重要，加上管理層在使用預期信貸虧損評估本
 and the involvement of subjective judgement and                集團交易應收款項的減值虧損時，涉及管理層的主觀判
 management estimates in evaluating the ECL of the Group’s      斷和估算。
 trade receivables at the end of the reporting period.

 As disclosed in note 40(b) to the consolidated financial       如綜合財務報表附註40(b)所披露，本集團管理層在考慮
 statements, management of the Group estimates the              賬齡及還款記錄後，通過對具有共同風險特徵的債務人
 amount of lifetime ECL of trade receivables based on a         進行分組，再根據撥備矩陣模型估算交易應收款項的全
 provision matrix through grouping of various debtors           期預期信貸虧損。估算損失率是基於債務人的預期壽命
 that have common risk characteristics, after considering       期間的歷史觀察違約率，並根據前瞻性信息進行調整。
 debtors’ aging, repayment history and past due status of       此外，信貸減值的交易應收款項或具有重大結餘會個別
 respective trade receivables. Estimated loss rates are based   評估。信貸減值的交易應收款項的撥備金額乃按資產賬
 on historical observed default rates over the expected         面值與估計未來現金流量現值之間的差額，並考慮預期
 life of the debtors and are adjusted for forward-looking       未來信貸虧損來計算。
 information. Trade receivables that are credit-impaired are
 separately assessed for ECL individually. The loss allowance
 amount of the credit-impaired trade receivables is measured
 as the difference between the asset’s gross amount and
 the present value of estimated future cash flows with the
 consideration of expected future credit losses.

            when considered together with other information included                   資料與實體財務報表所載其他

            policy information relating to material transactions, other                而，並非所有與重大交易、其
            events or conditions is itself material. If an entity chooses              他事項或情況有關之會計政策
            to disclose immaterial accounting policy information, such                 資料自身屬重大。倘實體選擇
            information must not obscure material accounting policy                    披露非重大會計政策資料，則
            information.                                                               有關資料不得掩蓋重大會計政
                                                                                       策資料。

            where the Company:                                                          以下事項，則視為取得控制權：

            (continued)
            Basis of consolidation (continued)                                           綜合基準（續）
            The Group reassesses whether or not it controls an investee                  倘有事實及情況顯示上文所列
            if facts and circumstances indicate that there are changes to                三項控制元素之一項或多項有
            one or more of the three elements of control listed above.                   變，則本集團會重估其是否控
                                                                                         制被投資方。

            Consolidation of a subsidiary begins when the Group                          本集團取得附屬公司控制權時
            obtains control over the subsidiaries and ceases when the                    開始將附屬公司綜合入賬，於
            Group loses control of the subsidiary. Specifically, income                  喪失附屬公司控制權時終止入
            and expenses of a subsidiary acquired or disposed of during                  賬。具體而言，年內收購或出
            the years are included in the consolidated statement of                      售之附屬公司收入及開支自本
            profit or loss and other comprehensive income from the                       集團取得控制權當日及直至本
            date the Group gains control until the date when the                         集團不再控制附屬公司之日止
            Group ceases to control the subsidiary.                                      計入綜合損益及其他全面收益
                                                                                         表。

            Profit or loss and each item of other comprehensive income                   損益及其他全面收入各項目歸
            are attributed to the owners of the Company and to the                       屬於本公司擁有人及非控股權
            non-controlling interests. Total comprehensive income of                     益。附屬公司之全面收入總額

            Investments in associates and joint ventures                                 於聯營公司及合營企業之投資
            An associate is an entity over which the Group has                           聯營公司為本集團對其有重大
            significant influence. Significant influence is the power to                 影響力之實體。重大影響力為
            participate in the financial and operating policy decisions                  有權參與被投資方之財務及營
            of the investee but is not control or joint control over those               運政策決定，惟並非控制或共
            policies.                                                                    同控制該等政策。

            A joint venture is a joint arrangement whereby the parties                   合營企業為對安排擁有共同控
            that have joint control of the arrangement have rights to                    制權之各方對共同安排之資產
            the net assets of the joint arrangement. Joint control is the                淨值擁有權利之共同安排。共
            contractually agreed sharing of control of an arrangement,                   同控制權為合約約定對安排所
            which exists only when decisions about the relevant                          共有之控制權，其僅在相關業
            activities require unanimous consent of the parties sharing                  務決策須取得共有控制權各方
            control.                                                                     之一致同意時存在。

            uniform accounting policies as those of the Group for like                   似情況下就同類交易及事項所

            the equity method, an investment in an associate or a joint                  權益法，於聯營公司或合營企

      3.2   Material accounting policy information                                 3.2   重大會計政策（續）
            (continued)
            Leases                                                                       租賃
            Definition of a lease                                                        租賃之定義
            A contract is, or contains, a lease if the contract conveys the              倘合約賦予權利於一段時間內
            right to control the use of an identified asset for a period of              控制已識別資產之用途以換取
            time in exchange for consideration.                                          代價，則該合約為租賃或包含
                                                                                         租賃。

            For contracts entered into or modified on or after the date                  於首次應用日期或之後訂立或
            of initial application of IFRS 16 or arising from business                   修訂或業務合併產生之合約，
            combinations, the Group assesses whether a contract is                       本集團根據國際財務報告準則
            or contains a lease based on the definition under IFRS                       第 16 號 項 下 之 定 義 ， 於 開 始
            16 at inception, modification date or acquisition date, as                   日、修訂日或收購日（如適用）
            appropriate. Such contract will not be reassessed unless                     評估合約是否為租賃或包含租
            the terms and conditions of the contract are subsequently                    賃。除非合約條款及條件於其
            changed.                                                                     後變更，否則不會對此類合約
                                                                                         進行重新評估。

            The Group as a lessee                                                        本集團作為承租人
            Allocation of consideration to components of a contract                      將對價分攤至合約各組成部分
            For a contract that contains a lease component and one or                    倘合約包含一個租賃成分及一
            more additional lease or non-lease components, the Group                     個或多個額外的租賃或非租賃
            allocates the consideration in the contract to each lease                    成分，則本集團應基於租賃成
            component on the basis of the relative stand-alone price of                  分之相關單獨價格及非租賃成
            the lease component and the aggregate stand-alone price                      分之單獨價格總和，將合約對
            of the non-lease components.                                                 價在各租賃成分之間進行分攤。

            The Group applies the practical expedient not to separate                    本集團亦應用可行權宜方法，
            non-lease components from lease component, and instead                       不從租賃成分中區分非租賃成
            account for the lease component and any associated non-                      分，而是將租賃成分及任何相
            lease components as a single lease component.                                關非租賃成分入賬列作單一租
                                                                                         賃成分。

            Short-term leases and leases of low-value assets                             短期租賃及低價值資產租賃
            The Group applies the short-term lease recognition                           對於租期自開始日期起計為
            exemption to leases of office and dormitories that have a                    十二個月或以內且不包含購買
            lease term of 12 months or less from the commencement                        選擇權之辦公室物業之租賃，
            date and do not contain a purchase option. It also applies                   本集團應用短期租賃確認豁
            the recognition exemption for lease of low-value assets.                     免。其對低價值資產之租賃亦
            Lease payments on short-term leases and leases of low-                       應用確認豁免。短期租賃及低
            value assets are recognised as expense on a straight-line                    價值資產租賃之租賃付款按直
            basis or another systematic basis over the lease term.                       線法或另一有系統基準於租期
                                                                                         內確認為開支。

            Taxation                                                                       稅項
            Income tax expense represents the sum of the tax currently                     所得稅開支指即期應付稅項與
            payable and deferred tax.                                                      遞延稅項之總和。

            The tax currently payable is based on taxable profit for the                   即期應付稅項按年內應課稅溢
            year. Taxable profit differs from “profit before tax” because                  利計算。由於應課稅溢利不包
            of income or expense that are taxable or deductible in other                   括其他年度應課稅或可扣稅之
            years and items that are never taxable or deductible. The                      收入或開支項目，亦不包括毋
            Group’s liability for current tax is calculated using tax rates                須課稅或不可扣稅項目，故應
            that have been enacted or substantively enacted by the end                     課稅溢利與於綜合損益及其他
            of the reporting period.                                                       全 面 收 益 表 呈 報 之「 除 稅 前 溢
                                                                                           利」不同。本集團之即期稅項負
                                                                                           債按於報告期間結算日已頒佈
                                                                                           或實質頒佈之稅率計算。

            Deferred tax is recognised on temporary differences                            遞延稅項按綜合財務報表內資
            between the carrying amounts of assets and liabilities in the                  產及負債賬面值與計算應課稅
            consolidated financial statements and the corresponding tax                    溢利所用相應稅基間之暫時差
            bases used in the computation of taxable profit. Deferred                      額確認。所有應課稅暫時差額
            tax liabilities are generally recognised for all taxable                       一般確認為遞延稅項負債。倘
            temporary differences. Deferred tax assets are generally                       應課稅溢利可能抵銷可扣減暫
            recognised for all deductible temporary differences to the                     時差額，則所有可扣減暫時差
            extent that it is probable that taxable profits will be available              額一般確認為遞延稅項資產。
            against which those deductible temporary differences can                       倘其他資產與負債之商譽或初
            be utilised. Such deferred tax assets and liabilities are not                  步確認（而非於業務合併中）於
            recognised if the temporary difference arises from goodwill                    交易中所產生之暫時差額不影
            or from the initial recognition (other than in a business                      響應課稅溢利或會計溢利，則
            combination) of other assets and liabilities in a transaction                  不會確認有關資產與負債並且
            that affects neither the taxable profit nor the accounting                     在交易時不會產生相等的應課
            profit and at the time of the transaction does not give rise                   稅和可抵減暫時性差異。此外，
            to equal taxable and deductible temporary differences. In                      如果暫時差額產生於商譽的初
            addition, deferred tax liabilities are not recognised if the                   步確認，則不確認遞延稅項負
            temporary difference arises from the initial recognition of                    債。
            goodwill.

            Deferred tax liabilities are recognised for taxable temporary                  於附屬公司及聯營公司之投資
            differences associated with investments in subsidiaries, and                   以及於合營企業之權益相關之
            interests in joint ventures and associates, except where                       應課稅暫時差額確認為遞延稅
            the Group is able to control the reversal of the temporary                     項負債，惟倘本集團能控制暫
            difference and it is probable that the temporary difference                    時差額之撥回，且有關暫時差
            will not reverse in the foreseeable future. Deferred tax assets                額可能不會於可見將來撥回則
            arising from deductible temporary differences associated                       除外。與該等投資及權益相關
            with such investments and interests are only recognised to                     之可扣稅暫時差額所產生遞延
            the extent that it is probable that there will be sufficient                   稅項資產，僅於可能有足夠應
            taxable profits against which to utilise the benefits of the                   課稅溢利可動用暫時差額之利
            temporary differences and they are expected to reverse in                      益且預計於可見將來可以撥回
            the foreseeable future.                                                        之情況下確認。

            The carrying amount of deferred tax assets is reviewed at                      本集團於各報告期間結算日會
            the end of the reporting period and reduced to the extent                      檢討遞延稅項資產之賬面值，
            that it is no longer probable that sufficient taxable profits                  並於不再可能有足夠應課稅溢
            will be available to allow all or part of the asset to be                      利收回全部或部分資產時扣減。
            recovered.

            Deferred tax assets and liabilities are measured at the tax                    遞延稅項資產及負債按預期於
            rates that are expected to apply in the period in which the                    清償負債或變現資產期間適用
            liability is settled or the asset is realised, based on tax rates              之稅率，以於報告期間結算日
            (and tax laws) that have been enacted or substantively                         已頒佈或實質頒佈之稅率（及稅
            enacted by the end of the reporting period.                                    法）為基準計量。

            The measurement of deferred tax liabilities and assets                         遞延稅項負債及資產之計量反
            reflects the tax consequences that would follow from the                       映於各報告期間結算日將自本
            manner in which the Group expects, at the end of each                          集團預期收回或清償其資產與
            reporting period, to recover or settle the carrying amount                     負債賬面值方式產生之稅務後
            of its assets and liabilities.                                                 果。

            be recovered entirely through sale, unless the presumption is                  該等房地產的賬面價值應推定
            rebutted. The presumption is rebutted when the investment                      為完全通過出售收回。當投資
            property is depreciable and is held within a business model                    性房地產可折舊，且其目標是
            whose objective is to consume substantially all of the                         隨著時間的推移而實質上消耗
            economic benefits embodied in the investment property                          投資性房地產所體現的所有經
            over time, rather than through sale.                                           濟利益，而不是通過出售，則
                                                                                           該假設被推翻。

            For the purposes of measuring deferred tax for leasing                         就計量本集團確認使用權資產

            determines whether the tax deductions are attributable to                      定減稅額應歸屬於使用權資產
            the right-of-use assets or the lease liabilities.                              或租賃負債。

            attributable to the lease liabilities, the Group applies IAS                   賃交易而言，本集團分別應用
            12 requirements to the lease liabilities and the related                       國際會計準則第12號所得稅有
            assets separately. The Group recognises a deferred tax                         關所有權資產及租賃負債之規
            asset related to lease liabilities to the extent that it is                    定。本集團確認有關租賃負債
            probable that taxable profit will be available against which                   的一項遞延稅項資產（以可能獲
            the deductible temporary difference can be utilised and a                      得可抵扣臨時稅差異的應課稅
            deferred tax liability for all taxable temporary differences.                  所得為限）及所有應課稅暫時差
                                                                                           異之遞延稅項負債。

            The effective interest method is a method of calculating                      實際利率法為計算金融資產或
            the amortised cost of a financial asset or financial liability                金融負債攤銷成本及於有關期
            and of allocating interest income and interest expense over                   間分配利息收入及利息開支之
            the relevant period. The effective interest rate is the rate                  方法。實際利率為於金融資產
            that exactly discounts estimated future cash receipts and                     或 金 融 負 債 預 計 年 期 或（ 如 適
            payments (including all fees and points paid or received                      用）較短期間內準確貼現估計未
            that form an integral part of the effective interest rate,                    來現金付款（包括構成實際利率
            transaction costs and other premiums or discounts) through                    其中一部分之所有已付或已收
            the expected life of the financial asset or financial liability,              費用及點子、交易成本及其他
            or, where appropriate, a shorter period, to the net carrying                  溢價或折讓）至初步確認時之賬
            amount on initial recognition.                                                面淨值之利率。

            assets

                   interest on the principal amount outstanding.                                 本金及未償還本金之利
                                                                                                 息。

            subsequently measured at fair value through other                              按公平值計入其他全面收益
            comprehensive income (“FVTOCI”):                                              （「 按 公 平 值 計 入 其 他 全 面 收
                                                                                           益」）計量：

                   whose objective is achieved by both selling and                               金流量達成目的之業務

            (v)    Measurement and recognition of ECL                                    (v)   計量及確認預期信貸虧

                   The measurement of ECL is a function of the                                 預期信貸虧損之計量乃

                   magnitude of the loss if there is a default) and                            率（ 即 違 約 造 成 虧 損 的
                   the exposure at default. The assessment of the                              幅 度 ）及 違 約 風 險 之 函
                   probability of default and loss given default is                            數。違約概率及違約虧
                   based on historical data and forward-looking                                損率乃根據經前瞻性資
                   information. Estimation of ECL reflects an unbiased                         料調整之歷史數據進行
                   and probability-weighted amount that is determined                          評估。預期信貸虧損之
                   with the respective risks of default occurring as the                       估計反映無偏頗及概率
                   weights. Trade receivables that are credit-impaired                         加權數額，其乃根據加
                   are assessed for ECL individually. In addition, the                         權之相應違約風險而釐
                   Group uses a practical expedient in estimating                              定。本集團經考慮過往

                   individually using a provision matrix taking into                           備矩陣並採用可行權宜
                   consideration historical credit loss experience,                            之計估計交易應收款項
                   adjusted for forward looking information that is                            之預期信貸虧損，並按
                   available without undue cost or effort.                                     毋需花費不必要成本或

                   to share similar credit risk characteristics.                              之組成項目繼續具有相

                   Interest income is calculated based on the gross                           利息收入乃基於金融資

            entity.                                                                     確認金融資產。

24. TRADE AND OTHER RECEIVABLES                                                  24. 交易及其他應收款項（續）
    (CONTINUED)
      Bills receivables at FVTOCI                                                    按公平值計入其他全面收益之應收
                                                                                     銀行票據
      As part of the Group’s cash flow management, the Group will                    作為本集團現金流量管理之一部份，本
      discount bills receivables to financial institutions or endorse                集團於到期付款前將應收銀行票據貼現
      bills receivables to suppliers before the due for payment, and                 轉讓予金融機構或將應收銀行票據背書
      derecognise the discounted and endorsed bills receivables on the               予供應商，並在本集團已基本轉移所有
      basis that the Group has transferred substantially all risks and               風險和回報予交易對方後，終止確認。
      rewards to the relevant counterparties. Thus, bills receivables at             因此，按公平值計入其他全面收益之應
      FVTOCI are managed within a business model whose objective is                  收銀行票據是在一個以收取合同現金流
      both to collect the contractual cash flows and to sell. Therefore,             量和出售為目標的隨後商業模式下進行
      these bills receivables were subsequently classified as debt                   管理。因此，該等應收銀行票據於採用
      instruments at FVTOCI upon adoption of IFRS 9.                                 國際財務報告準則第9號分類為按公平
                                                                                     值計入其他全面收益之債務工具。

      The following is an aged analysis of bills receivables at FVTOCI, net          下表為在報告期末按公平值計入其他全
      of fair value remeasurement under the requirement of IFRS 9, at                面收益之應收銀行票據（扣除國際財務
      the end of the reporting period:                                               報告準則第9號規定項下之公平值重新
                                                                                     計量）之帳齡分析：

                                                                                             2023                2022
                                                                                          二零二三年              二零二二年
                                                                                          RMB’000             RMB’000
                                                                                          人民幣千元              人民幣千元

        0 to 180 days                               0至180日                                    72,366             257,848

      The bills receivables at FVTOCI are all issued by reputable banks              按公平值計入其他全面收益之應收銀行
      of good credit quality. The management of the Group considered                 票據全部均由信用良好之銀行發行。本
      the credit risk of these bank issued bills is insignificant and no             集團管理層認為該等銀行票據的信用風
      impairment was provided on them at the year end.                               險極低，故年末未有提減值準備。

39. CAPITAL RISK MANAGEMENT                                                   39. 資本風險管理
      The Group manages its capital to ensure that entities in the Group        本集團管理其資本以確保本集團內之實
      will be able to continue as a going concern while maximising the          體能夠持續經營，並透過優化債務及權
      return to shareholders through the optimisation of the debt and           益結餘盡量增加股東回報。本集團整體
      equity balance. The Group’s overall strategy remains unchanged            策略於有關期間維持不變。
      throughout the relevant periods.

      The capital structure of the Group consists of net debt that includes     本集團之資本架構包含債務淨額（包括
      the borrowings and lease liabilities disclosed in note 30 and note        分別於附註30及31披露之借貸及租賃
      31 respectively, net of bank balances and equity attributable to          負債），扣除銀行結餘及本公司擁有人
      owners of the Company, comprising issued share capital, reserves          應佔權益（包括已發行股本、儲備及保
      and retained earnings.                                                    留溢利）。

      The directors of the Company review the capital structure from            董事不時檢討資本架構。作為檢討之一
      time to time. As a part of this review, the directors of the Company      部分，董事考慮資本成本及各類資本相
      consider the cost of capital and the risks associated with each           關之風險。本集團將根據董事建議，透
      class of capital. Based on recommendations of the directors of            過派付股息、發行新股、新造債務或贖
      the Company, the Group will balance its overall capital structure         回現有債務，平衡其整體資本架構。
      through the payment of dividends, the issue of new shares, new
      debts or the redemption of existing debts.

            Financial assets at FVTOCI were all bills receivables. Since               按公平值計入其他全面收益之
            the issuers were reputable banks of good credit quality,                   金融資產均為應收銀行票據。
            the management of the Group considered the credit risk of                  由於發行人為信用良好之銀
            these bank issued bills is insignificant and no impairment                 行，本集團管理層認為該等銀
            was provided on them at the year end.                                      行發行票據之信用風險極低，
                                                                                       故年末未計提減值準備。

            Amounts due from an associate and a joint venture                          應收一間聯營公司及一間合營
                                                                                       企業款項
            No allowances for credit losses was made to these balances                 由於本集團在評估對手之財務
            since the Group consider the probability of default is                     背景及可信度後認為違約之可
            minimal after assessing the counter-parties’ financial                     能性極低，故並無作出減值撥
            background and creditability.                                              備。

            Restricted bank deposits/bank balances                                     受限制銀行存款╱銀行結餘
            The credit risk on restricted bank deposits/bank balances is               由於對手方為信譽良好銀行或
            limited because the counterparties are reputable banks or                  金融機構，故受限制銀行存
            financial institutions.                                                    款╱銀行結餘之信貸風險有限。
